  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Shaka, leader of The Zulus (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: The Zulus
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Impi, Replacing Tercio (Requires technology: Gunpowder)
        - Unique Unit: InDuna, Replacing Great General (Requires technology: None)
        - Unique Building: Ikanda, Replacing Barracks (Requires technology: Bronze Working)
        - Unique Building: Iziko, Replacing Amphitheater (Requires technology: Drama and Poetry)
        - Civilization Ability: -50% Gold maintenance for Melee and Gunpowder Units. All Units require 25% less Experience for Promotions. Your military ignores Alliance and Protection penalties and is 50% more effective at intimidating City-States.
      - Leader: Shaka
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Nuclear Fission: Allows you to build the Bazooka, the most powerful ranged Unit and Atomic Bombs, the first nuclear weapon in the game (note: you must first have completed the Manhattan Project and have Uranium available). Also allows you to build the Nuclear Plant, a building which requires Uranium and increases the Production of a City.
        Completing it would unlock: Satellites, Advanced Ballistics
      - Refrigeration: Allows Work Boats to construct an Offshore Platform, and increases Trade Route maximum travel distance.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics, Computers
      
      ## Policies
      - Dictatorship of the Proletariat (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Dictatorship of the Proletariat: +7 Food, Science, Gold, and Culture per city.
      - Five-Year Plan (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Five-Year Plan: +20% Production towards Buildings in all Cities. +3 Production for every Mine, Quarry, Lumber Mill, Oil Well, and Unique Improvement.
      - Resettlement (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Resettlement: New Cities have +3 Population. All Cities gain +2 Citizens immediately. No Partisans from razing enemy Cities.
      - Cultural Revolution (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Cultural Revolution: +25% Tourism to civilizations with less Happiness. +1 Happiness from Factories.
      - Great Leap Forward (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Great Leap Forward: Receive a free Technology. Spies gain +100% Science. +1 Science in Cities for every 3 non-Specialist Citizens.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Exploitation (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Food and +1 Production from Farms, Camps, and Plantations.
        - * +1 Production and Science from Coasts, Lakes, and Oceans.
      - Hero of the People (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Hero of the People: Great Person rate increases by 25%. A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Rights of Man (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Villages.
        - * +10% Science during Golden Ages.
      - Free Thought (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -5 Unhappiness from Religious Unrest in all Cities.
        - * +25% Instant Yields from Great Scientists.
        - * Earn Great Scientists +33% faster.
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Splendor (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Expending a Great Person grants 50 Culture, scaling with Era.
        - * State Treasury built in Capital (+4 Gold, +2 Culture to all Monuments, Gardens, and Baths; 1 Merchant Slot).
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Organization (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Production in every City.
        - * +25% Production toward Workers and Trade Units.
        - * +1 Movement for Workers.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Foreign Service (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 100 Spy Points.
        - * Earn Great Diplomats 50% faster.
        - * +50% Rewards from City-State Quests.
        - * +1 of every Strategic Resource for every three City-State Alliances you maintain.
      - Nationalization (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Nationalization: Each Corporate Office counts as a Franchise. Foreign Franchises no longer benefit your Corporation, you can no longer construct Franchises in foreign Cities (except Vassals), and foreign Corporations can no longer set up Franchises in your Cities (except Masters). +2 Science from all Corporation Offices.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

Synergizes with immediate terminal assault phase - every production point must contribute to Amsterdam/Cusco capture before Dutch cultural victory.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Assyria
        - Public: 0

Truly neutral stance - may become free if Inca weakens, or irrelevant target later. No diplomatic investment needed while focused on total war.

Focus now on integrating Warsaw (Courthouse, buildings, happiness management). Former -20/-25 hostility irrelevant as city now under direct Zulu control.

        ### Sydney

        - UpdatedTurn: 320
        
        ### Antwerp
        - Public: 0

        ### Helsinki
        - Public: 10
        - Private: 10
        - Rationale: HELSINKI PEACE SECURED: Peace established turn 297 maintained. Reduces war count from potential 5 to manageable level. Helsinki is Inca ally (188 influence) and Inca sphere proposal likely passed - no benefit to re-engaging. Focus on major capitals only.
        - UpdatedTurn: 320
        
        ### Sidon
        - Public: -95

        - UpdatedTurn: 320
        
        ### Budapest

        - UpdatedTurn: 320
        
        ### Oslo
        - Public: -60
        - Private: -70

        - UpdatedTurn: 320
        
        ### Morocco
        - Public: -25
        - Private: -30
        - Rationale: MOROCCO IRRELEVANT - ZERO RESOURCES: Inca's vassal (1 city, 582 military). Technically at war but no strategic threat. Will become free if Inca falls. Maintain hostility but dedicate zero military resources - all forces to Inca/Netherlands capitals only. Not worth diverting attention from existential Dutch threat.
        - UpdatedTurn: 371
        
        ### Ethiopia
        - Public: 50
        - Private: 50
        - Rationale: ETHIOPIA VASSAL STABLE: Vassal contributing tribute and delegates. They maintain eastern border security. No issues - continue positive relations as they provide stable income and WC votes without military commitment.
        - UpdatedTurn: 376
        
        ### Byzantium
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: BYZANTIUM VOTING BLOC: 6-7 delegates essential despite -999 ideology penalty. 100/100 overrides for WC coordination. Historically voted with us against Dutch proposals. With Ottoman 33 + our 10 + Byzantium 6-7 = ~50 delegates, can block Dutch hegemony attempts. They are fighting Ottomans but we maintain coordination for WC only.
        - UpdatedTurn: 393
        
        ### The Inca
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: INCA WAR ACTIVE - MAXIMUM HOSTILITY MAINTAINED: Inca military strength 17,264 vs our 8,059 requires full commitment. They control Cusco and Marrakech (2 capitals needed for Domination). Defensive Pact with Netherlands makes them immediate threat. War declared T388, now prosecuting. Four spies include Agent Jama in Cusco (network 1229) gathering intel/pressuring. No peace until Cusco captured.
        - UpdatedTurn: 400
        
        ### The Netherlands
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: NETHERLANDS EXISTENTIAL THREAT - MAXIMUM HOSTILITY: Citizen Earth Protocol construction imminent. 155% cultural influence on us, 6/6 influential civs. Only domination can stop cultural victory now. Spies Phesheya (Amsterdam, network 242), Balungile (Nijmegen, network 156), Gonothi (Breda, network 0) actively sabotaging. War declared - four destroyers/submarines engaging Dutch navy. Must capture Amsterdam/Assur before victory triggers.
        - UpdatedTurn: 400
        
        ### The Ottomans
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: OTTOMAN ALLIANCE MAINTAINED: Turn 404 - Ottomans have 47 delegates, we have 9, totaling 56 vs Dutch 27. They oppose Netherlands culturally (128% influence) and militarily (war declared). They possess Uranium (traded 45 for 8 GPT) and completed Apollo Program. Critical for World Congress coordination to block Dutch proposals. Information Era military parity essential - do not disrupt alliance while prosecuting simultaneous Amsterdam/Cusco assaults.
        - UpdatedTurn: 404
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: TERMINAL ASSAULT PHASE - TURN 404 CRISIS: Netherlands cultural victory imminent (155% influence, Citizen Earth Protocol likely constructing). Inca war continues with Defensive Pact backing Netherlands. Nuclear threshold increased (Nuke 100, UseNuke 60) - Atomic Theory research enables Manhattan Project path. Maximum offensive flavors sustained: Offense 100, Mobilization 100, Air 100, Mobile 100 drive Heavy Bomber/Mechanized Infantry production for simultaneous Amsterdam/Cusco assaults. Naval 85 maintains destroyer/submarine pressure. Espionage 100 sustains spy networks (Amsterdam 408, Cusco 1322). Science 100 prioritizes nuclear path. No retreat until capitals captured - existential threat requires total war commitment.

        ## DiplomaticVictory
        - VotesNeeded: 44
        - Status: World Congress - Voting in 6 turns
        - ActiveResolutions:
          - Casus Belli:
            - Description: The world recognizes the need for justifiable war to resolve diplomatic disputes.
              While this resolution is active, unit maintenance costs are lowered by 15% Gold. 0.5x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 2x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - World Religion: Protestantism:
            - Description: Designate a religion as the official World Religion. Civilizations who have a majority of the cities in their empire following the religion get an additional Delegate. The founder of the religion and/or the controller of its Holy City receives additional votes based on the number of Civilizations following the religion. The religion spreads 25% faster, and its Holy City receives +50% Tourism. There can only be one World Religion at a time.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Cultural Heritage Sites:
            - Description: Each World Wonder provides +2 Culture to its city, and each Great Work provides +1 Science to its city.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Dutch Sphere of Influence: Enns:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Incan Sphere of Influence: Helsinki:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Cold War:
            - Description: Ideological differences have pushed the world into a cold war, and economic and political factions are forming based on ideology alone.
              While this resolution is active, players may only make Trade Routes with civilizations that follow the same ideology, or with allied City-States. Furthermore, Influence levels with allied City-States do not decline.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Atomic Theory.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Incan Sphere of Influence: Antananarivo:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - World Ideology: Order:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Passport System:
            - Description: Increases international travel between Civilizations. Increases the Tourism modifier for all Civilizations by 50%.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Flight.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Incan Sphere of Influence: Wanggeom:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
        - Proposals:
          - Enact: Historical Landmarks:
            - Description: Great Person tile improvements provide +1 to Food, Production, and Gold when worked, and Landmarks built by Archaeologists provide +1 Science, Culture, and Faith when worked.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Archaeology.
            - Proposer: The Ottomans
          - Enact: Decolonization:
            - Description: Enforce global decolonization. If successful, the Influence of all Players with all City-States is lowered to 50, unless they are permanent allies.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Radio.
            - Proposer: The Netherlands
          - Repeal: World Ideology: Order:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - Proposer: The Inca

        You, Shaka (leader of The Zulus, Player 2), are making strategic decisions after turn 405.
- replay:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Shaka, leader of The Zulus (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: The Zulus
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Impi, Replacing Tercio (Requires technology: Gunpowder)
        - Unique Unit: InDuna, Replacing Great General (Requires technology: None)
        - Unique Building: Ikanda, Replacing Barracks (Requires technology: Bronze Working)
        - Unique Building: Iziko, Replacing Amphitheater (Requires technology: Drama and Poetry)
        - Civilization Ability: -50% Gold maintenance for Melee and Gunpowder Units. All Units require 25% less Experience for Promotions. Your military ignores Alliance and Protection penalties and is 50% more effective at intimidating City-States.
      - Leader: Shaka
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Nuclear Fission: Allows you to build the Bazooka, the most powerful ranged Unit and Atomic Bombs, the first nuclear weapon in the game (note: you must first have completed the Manhattan Project and have Uranium available). Also allows you to build the Nuclear Plant, a building which requires Uranium and increases the Production of a City.
        Completing it would unlock: Satellites, Advanced Ballistics
      - Refrigeration: Allows Work Boats to construct an Offshore Platform, and increases Trade Route maximum travel distance.
        Completing it would unlock: Electronics, Computers
      
      ## Policies
      - Dictatorship of the Proletariat (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Dictatorship of the Proletariat: +7 Food, Science, Gold, and Culture per city.
      - Five-Year Plan (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Five-Year Plan: +20% Production towards Buildings in all Cities. +3 Production for every Mine, Quarry, Lumber Mill, Oil Well, and Unique Improvement.
      - Resettlement (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Resettlement: New Cities have +3 Population. All Cities gain +2 Citizens immediately. No Partisans from razing enemy Cities.
      - Cultural Revolution (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Cultural Revolution: +25% Tourism to civilizations with less Happiness. +1 Happiness from Factories.
      - Great Leap Forward (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Great Leap Forward: Receive a free Technology. Spies gain +100% Science. +1 Science in Cities for every 3 non-Specialist Citizens.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Exploitation (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Food and +1 Production from Farms, Camps, and Plantations.
        - * +1 Production and Science from Coasts, Lakes, and Oceans.
      - Hero of the People (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Hero of the People: Great Person rate increases by 25%. A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Rights of Man (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Villages.
        - * +10% Science during Golden Ages.
      - Free Thought (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -5 Unhappiness from Religious Unrest in all Cities.
        - * +25% Instant Yields from Great Scientists.
        - * Earn Great Scientists +33% faster.
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Splendor (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Expending a Great Person grants 50 Culture, scaling with Era.
        - * State Treasury built in Capital (+4 Gold, +2 Culture to all Monuments, Gardens, and Baths; 1 Merchant Slot).
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Organization (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Production in every City.
        - * +25% Production toward Workers and Trade Units.
        - * +1 Movement for Workers.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Foreign Service (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 100 Spy Points.
        - * Earn Great Diplomats 50% faster.
        - * +50% Rewards from City-State Quests.
        - * +1 of every Strategic Resource for every three City-State Alliances you maintain.
      - Nationalization (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Order): Nationalization: Each Corporate Office counts as a Franchise. Foreign Franchises no longer benefit your Corporation, you can no longer construct Franchises in foreign Cities (except Vassals), and foreign Corporations can no longer set up Franchises in your Cities (except Masters). +2 Science from all Corporation Offices.
      - Fealty (New Branch):
        - Fealty enhances your ability to defend and leverage your Majority Religion.
        - Adopting Fealty grants:
        - * Can purchase Monasteries with Faith (+3 Food, +3 Science, +2 Faith).
        - * -25% Faith costs for purchasing Buildings, Missionaries, and Inquisitors.
        - Each Fealty policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Faith and Strength in every City.
        - Adopting all Policies in Fealty grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Red Fort.
        - * Your Tourism modifier for Shared Religion is 50% stronger.
        - * Cities that follow your Majority Religion generate +3 Production, Gold, Science, and Culture.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Artists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
      - Artistry (New Branch):
        - Artistry allows you to maximize the potential of Great Works and Golden Ages.
        - Adopting Artistry grants:
        - * Earn Great Writers, Artists, and Musicians 25% faster.
        - * +10% Culture during Golden Ages.
        - * +100% Production towards all Guilds.
        - Each Artistry policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Science in every City.
        - * 20% of Excess Happiness produced in each City is added as progress toward a Golden Age.
        - Adopting all policies in Artistry grants:
        - * Unlocks building the Louvre.
        - * Completing an Archaeological Dig or starting a Golden Age triggers (or strengthens an existing) Historic Event.
        - * Allows you to see Hidden Antiquity Sites.
        - * +3 Science from Landmarks.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Musicians with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Rationale: ATOMIC AGE WEAPONIZATION: Researching Nuclear Fission next to unlock the Manhattan Project, Atomic Bombs, and Nuclear Plants. With the Netherlands extremely close to a cultural victory, we need the ultimate tactical and strategic deterrent to obliterate their coastal defenses in Amsterdam and secure our domination path.
        - Technology: Nuclear Fission
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Rationale: WAR ECONOMY PRODUCTION AMPLIFICATION: Order level 2 tenet Five-Year Plan provides +20% Production toward Buildings and +3 Production for every Mine, Quarry, Lumber Mill, and Oil Well. With multiple captured cities and heavy bomber production in Ulundi, maximizing raw production is our absolute priority.
        - Policy: Five-Year Plan
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Public: 100
        - Rationale: OTTOMAN IRONCLAD ALLIANCE: Maintain maximum positive modifiers (100/100) with Suleiman. They are our critical partner in the World Congress with 49 delegates, and they are actively fighting our mutual enemies (Inca and Netherlands) on the eastern front.
        - Private: 100
        - TargetID: 3
    - #3:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: DUTCH EXISTENTIAL WAR: Maintain maximum hostility (-100/-100) to ensure our AI commits fully to attacking and capturing Amsterdam before their imminent cultural victory triggers.
        - TargetID: 5
        - Private: -100
        - Public: -100
    - #4:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Public: -100
        - TargetID: 0
        - Private: -100
        - Rationale: INCA CONQUEST PREPARATION: Maintain maximum hostility (-100/-100). They are allied with the Netherlands via a defensive pact and hold Cusco and Marrakech. We must plan for their eventual destruction and capture of Cusco.
    - #5:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Private: -100
        - TargetID: 1
        - Rationale: ETHIOPIAN ELIMINATION: Update from obsolete vassal state to active war hostility. We are besieging their final city, Awasa (at 0% health), to eliminate them from the map and free up southern forces.
        - Public: -100
    - #6:
      - toolName: set-flavors
      - args:
        - Flavors:
          - Science: 100
          - Diplomacy: 15
          - Offense: 100
          - Naval: 85
          - Ranged: 90
          - Happiness: 30
          - Nuke: 100
          - Air: 100
          - UseNuke: 80
          - Mobile: 100
          - Mobilization: 100
          - Espionage: 100
          - Production: 100
          - Defense: 10
          - MilitaryTraining: 95
          - GreatPeople: 30
          - AirCarrier: 80
        - GrandStrategy: Conquest
      - rationale: CONQUEST FLAVOR REFINEMENT: Maintain maximum offense, mobilization, air, and naval flavors to push on Amsterdam. Increased UseNuke to 80 to prepare the tactical AI for nuclear deployment once Nuclear Fission completes. Increased Happiness to 30 to prompt the AI to build courthouses and resolve unhappiness in captured cities like Bahir Dar.
  - tokens:
    - inputTokens: 63505
    - reasoningTokens: 6135
    - outputTokens: 600
  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Analyzing Domination Progress**
            
            I'm currently assessing our Domination victory path. The Zulus hold control over two of their original territories, and I'm strategizing the next offensive moves.
            
            **Evaluating Victory Conditions**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our paths to victory. The Zulus maintain control over two of their original capitals, but I've spotted a critical threat: the Netherlands are nearing a Cultural Victory, having influenced all other civilizations. I'm now investigating the current war status with Ethiopia, as it seems we are actively engaged.
            
            **Assessing Immediate Threats**
            
            My focus has shifted to the Netherlands. They're on the cusp of a Cultural Victory, having influenced all other civs, but need the Citizen Earth Protocol. We're currently at war with them, so I'm analyzing how best to disrupt their progress while also considering our ongoing conflict with the Inca.
            
            **Clarifying Diplomatic Entanglements**
            
            I'm untangling our war status with the Inca and Netherlands. It appears we're at war with the Netherlands, but the Inca only plan to attack. Their defensive pact with the Dutch is a confusing factor. I'm verifying if the Inca has been pulled into our conflict with the Netherlands.
            
            **Investigating Inca Involvement**
            
            I'm clarifying the Inca's non-involvement in our war with the Netherlands despite their defensive pact. Ethiopia is down to one city and minimal strength, so they pose no threat. I'm continuing research on Atomic Theory.
            
            **Selecting Nuclear Fission**
            
            I've decided to prioritize researching Nuclear Fission. This advancement is crucial for unlocking atomic bombs, which will be instrumental in disrupting the Netherlands' potential Cultural Victory and achieving our own conquest goals.
            
            **Choosing Next Policy**
            
            I am currently reviewing our next policy selection. We are six turns from acquiring a new one, and I'm verifying if our previously considered `Five-Year Plan` has already been adopted. I need to determine the optimal path forward from the available options.
            
            **Confirming Policy Selection**
            
            I'm confirming our next policy as `Five-Year Plan`. Its production bonuses are essential for our industrial war efforts. I've also analyzed our alliances and rivalries, noting the critical war with the Netherlands and Inca. Ethiopia is essentially neutralized.
            
            **Analyzing Ethiopian Status**
            
            I'm clarifying Ethiopia's current status. Despite a previous vassalage, they are now at war with us, and our units are advancing on Awasa. I'm confirming the ongoing invasion and noting that their previous "vassal stable" status is outdated.
            
            **Conquering Final Territories**
            
            I'm finalizing our conquest strategy. Ethiopia is on the brink of elimination as we lay siege to their last city. I'm also assessing Morocco, an Inca vassal, and preparing for potential conflict as we advance our war plans. Our focus remains firmly on offensive and military production.
            
            **Voting Against Repeal**
            
            I'm focusing on the upcoming World Congress vote in six turns. The Inca propose repealing World Ideology: Order, which would be disastrous for our happiness. I'm also analyzing the Netherlands' "Decolonization" proposal and its impact on our Ottoman allies.
            
            **Securing World Ideology**
            
            I'm confirming our voting strength in the World Congress. With 58 delegates through our alliance with the Ottomans, we command a clear majority. We will prioritize voting against the Inca's repeal of World Ideology: Order and the Netherlands' "Decolonization" proposal.
            
            **Defending Ottoman Ally**
            
            I'm analyzing our military status near Bahir Dar, where Dutch naval forces threaten our Ottoman ally's city of Budapest. We've lost a cruiser to their submarines but are upgrading units and rebasing aircraft to counter the threat. Our own production is advancing well.
            
            **Prioritizing City Happiness**
            
            I'm focusing on resolving Bahir Dar's critical happiness deficit of -21. Without a courthouse, the occupied city penalty is crippling production. My priority is now to ensure the AI builds a courthouse there immediately to stabilize our situation and boost our production capacity.
            
            **Adjusting Happiness Settings**
            
            I'm adjusting our global Happiness setting to 30 to encourage AI construction of necessary happiness buildings, especially courthouses in newly acquired cities. This complements our Conquest strategy by mitigating occupied city penalties and boosting overall production, despite our current war footing.
            
            **Updating Diplomatic Status**
            
            I'm updating our diplomatic stance to maximum hostility with the Netherlands and Inca, our primary targets. Ethiopia is also now at maximum hostility as we invade their last city. I've confirmed Byzantium is no longer in play. I'm also setting our flavor preferences to prioritize conquest.
            
            **Refining Actionable Tools**
            
            I'm reviewing our available tactical tools. I've confirmed that `set-relationship` is appropriate for the Ottomans, Netherlands, Inca, and Ethiopia, and `set-research` for Nuclear Fission. The `Five-Year Plan` policy and flavor adjustments are also confirmed, aligning with our aggressive conquest strategy.
            
            **Securing Critical Production**
            
            I'm prioritizing the "Five-Year Plan" policy for its production bonuses, essential for our war economy and heavy bomber output. I'm also solidifying our alliance with the Ottomans and cementing maximum hostility with the Netherlands, ensuring their full commitment to attacking Amsterdam.
